Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The recognition variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 621 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 268 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by recognition generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 621, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 268. QED.

This completes the proof. QED.
